# Shed ventilation implementation plan

Status: Approved

Date: 2026-10-12

## Authority and final outcome

Approved specification: `specs/SPEC-shed-ventilation.md`, complete 2026-10-12
revision, approved by user reply "approve".
SPEC SHA-256: `74fe2747d143fa5f483d767828580e41aade941cba249995b988927b65e25d94`.

Deliver one OpenSCAD source for the complete print-in-place vent, including its
captive hinge, full-panel downward opening, top catch and 90-degree stop, plus
README instructions. Preserve all approved dimensions and their provisional
provenance. No physical assembly is required; cleanup, freeing the hinge and
mounting screws are as specified. This PLAN introduces no behavior delta and
changes no earlier approved artifact. PLAN approval is separate from SPEC
approval; implementation requires a fresh session, cleared context, or explicit
same-context consent and valid implement entry.

## Evidence and bounded source approach

Planning checkout: `/home/alexbanica/workspace/openscad-designs`, local `main`
at `2d67bad90145c5d0d68fbd1ee430aa1990f8f3e0`. At inspection the only untracked
path was the new SPEC. OpenSCAD is available at `/usr/bin/openscad`. The remote
is `origin` (`git@github.com:alexbanica/openscad-designs.git`). These observations
are not a pinned execution base or proof of current remote state.

No existing shed model or library dependency is needed. Follow the repository's
parameter/module conventions; fixed slot examples in
`designs/linksys_lgs105_cable_management_enclosure.scad` and hinge transforms in
`designs/nested_bank_safe.scad` are bounded reference patterns only, not geometry
to copy wholesale or edit. Existing designs and specifications are outside
ownership. Do not reopen broad research during implementation.

Use closed-position printing with both the frame rear face and the door's
underside reaching Z=0. Build the door underside into the frame aperture with
clearance; build its overlapping perimeter above the frame with a printable
relief gap. Integral journals grow from the door and are captured by frame
bearing features, with no isolated floating axle. Use self-supporting bearing
roof geometry and accessible exterior supports where necessary; no trapped
support or sacrificial internal assembly feature. All moving door details use
one hinge-axis transform. Top catch flexure, stop contact, and screw keepouts
must be designed together with this envelope. In the as-printed closed pose,
all frame-to-door interfaces, including catch, stop, journals and retention
features, must have positive separation. A catch may retain the door behind a
clearanced lip without touching it in this pose; intentional operating contact
does not permit fused print geometry. Allocate numeric hinge offsets, bearing
wall thickness, retention overlap, overlap relief, stop and catch envelopes during U1 and check
them against the approved plate, overlap, clearance and opening before finishing
the geometry. These derived implementation dimensions do not replace SPEC inputs.

This is a geometric approach to validate, not proven printability. Do not deliver
an elevated floating door, fused hinge, obstructed passage, or a disconnected
pin to satisfy an export. If geometry contradicts the SPEC, use the implement
amendment process; do not silently weaken one-print/no-assembly behavior.
Reversible local defaults: descriptive module names, modest preview tessellation,
and temporary artifact names; none may change the approved dimensions/behavior.

## Workspace, base and delivery

Implementation worktree: `/tmp/agent-worktrees/openscad-designs/shed-ventilation`.
Create detached from freshly fetched `origin/main`; record the resolved SHA before
source changes. Exact delivery branch is local `main`, remote `origin/main`,
per repository instructions. No task-branch delivery substitutes for main.

The parent snapshots HEAD, index/content state and SHA-256 hashes of both approved
artifacts, then copies them byte-for-byte into the detached worktree. Preserve
all unrelated invoking-checkout changes. Refresh with
`git fetch origin +refs/heads/main:refs/remotes/origin/main`; failed fetch,
main divergence or unsafe checkout state blocks execution as DRAFT. Recheck
AGENTS.md at the fetched base. An absent worktree path is normal; an exactly
registered but absent planned path may be removed non-forcibly and recreated.
Other dirty/conflicting occupancy stops; never prune unrelated worktrees.

Following acceptance, parent reconciles all owned paths and original artifact
copies and safely updates invoking local main to the verified upstream, using
fast-forward only where needed. Transfer accepted source, README and artifact
content to main without overwriting concurrent work. The new SCAD and README
must be absent/unchanged respectively against the recorded execution base unless
an explicit reconciled integration resolves the difference. Stage only the four
owned paths, inspect the staged diff, commit on local main and push explicitly
with `git push origin main:main`. PLAN approval plus valid implement invocation
will authorize this complete lifecycle; no Git delivery is authorized by planning
alone. Verify remote main contains the accepted commit using fetched ref/remote
SHA, and verify upstream is origin/main. Do not push unrelated ahead commits.

Retain recovery state until transfer and remote verification finish. Remove only
the clean reconciled temporary worktree, without force. A rejected push must not
be forced: fetch, reassess and safely integrate if within authority; otherwise
retain an evidenced DRAFT. Incomplete work remains recoverable in its worktree;
do not put a blocked or unaccepted geometry change onto main merely to checkpoint.

## Delivery units and ownership

All substantive specialist assignments use fresh context, exact artifact hashes,
bounded authority and a five-minute maximum (aim three). Parent integrates and
owns Git, requirement decisions and acceptance. Specialists never branch, create
worktrees, commit or push. Serialize source ownership and repository checks;
follow-up units retain exclusive ownership after reconciling the previous worker.

| Unit | Prerequisites | Owner and paths | Completion / next step |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| U1 Geometry | Approved pair, valid implement entry, verified worktree/base | Developer: `designs/shed_ventilation.scad` only | Parameters/assertions, frame, eight-louver door, captive hinge, catch, stop, printable layout and assembly angles; development checks below. Then U2 |
| U2 Integrated assurance and corrections | U1 frozen source/diff and development evidence | Independent code-reviewer read-only; developer corrects same SCAD path after parent disposition | One integrated review wave, consolidated supported corrections, affected checks; then U3 |
| U3 Geometry QA | Corrected U2 snapshot | Independent qa-agent, read-only source; outputs under `/tmp` | Assess motion/retention, bed support and passage evidence, not merely repeat parsing; resolve qualifying failures through bounded corrections and affected QA; then U4 |
| U4 Documentation | Accepted geometry and QA dispositions | Parent: `README.md`; parent owns only approval/evidence updates to this PLAN and paired SPEC | Durable dimensions/provenance, one-print preparation, modes, tuning, installation and physical limits; inspect consistency; then U5 |
| U5 Acceptance and delivery | U1–U4 complete | Parent: integration/staging of the four owned paths only | Final diff check, DoD, commit on main, verified origin/main push, safe reconciliation/cleanup |

Checkpoints report completed work, next unit and actual commit/push state; unit
handoffs and checkpoint reports do not pause implementation for permission.

## Assurance profile

Select **routine**: bounded mechanical source and documentation with no changed
shared software interfaces, executable dependencies, deployment, security or
credentials. Independent integrated code review is required for geometry,
parameter consistency, regression isolation, print-in-place topology and motion.
Contract and security reviewers are inapplicable: no API/schema/integration or
security behavior is changed. Physical screw/clearance interfaces are covered by
code review and geometry QA, with measured fit explicitly deferred by the user.

Independent QA is required despite routine profile because the captive hinge,
overlap/bed-contact geometry and unobstructed sweep need evidence beyond source
review. Main synchronizes documentation under the routine profile. Unit tests and
conventional test-first phases are prohibited and inapplicable. Correct supported
findings within approved behavior, rerun affected checks, and repeat a cycle only
for confirmed failed acceptance, regression or blocked requested behavior. An
unresolved mandatory check or material geometry issue prevents acceptance.

## Validation commands and evidence

Use `timeout -k 1s 14s` around each OpenSCAD invocation so a background render
cannot continue beyond 15 seconds. All outputs go under
`/tmp/shed-ventilation-validation/`. No generated mesh enters Git.

Example command shapes, using the eventual declared `door_angle_deg` parameter:

```sh
mkdir -p /tmp/shed-ventilation-validation
timeout -k 1s 14s openscad -o /tmp/shed-ventilation-validation/layout.csg -D 'render_mode="printable_layout"' designs/shed_ventilation.scad
timeout -k 1s 14s openscad -o /tmp/shed-ventilation-validation/open.csg -D 'render_mode="assembly"' -D 'door_angle_deg=90' designs/shed_ventilation.scad
timeout -k 1s 14s openscad -o /tmp/shed-ventilation-validation/layout.stl -D 'render_mode="printable_layout"' designs/shed_ventilation.scad
timeout -k 1s 14s openscad -o /tmp/shed-ventilation-validation/closed.png --imgsize=1200,900 --viewall --autocenter -D 'render_mode="assembly"' -D 'door_angle_deg=0' designs/shed_ventilation.scad
git diff --check
```

Repeat assembly preview/CSG at 0, 30, 60 and 90 degrees, and create a printable
layout preview. Use offscreen Qt or an available virtual display for PNG renders.
Inspect images; parsing/CSG alone is not visual evidence. Record command outcomes,
paths and observations. Timeouts remain failed/incomplete evidence; do not extend
the limit. A substitute needs equivalent documented coverage under implement
amendment rules; required geometry validation still missing means blocked DRAFT.

Check each moving body's lowest geometry and first-layer connectivity, not only
the complete object's minimum Z. U1 development and U3 QA must explicitly check
positive frame-to-door separation at every moving interface in the as-printed
pose, including the catch; operating-contact exceptions never apply to that
pose. Confirm positive journal/bearing gaps, captive
retention across the angle range, no unintended collisions except deliberate
catch/stop contacts, and stop engagement at 90 degrees. Assess intermediate sweep
analytically from envelopes in addition to sampled views. Check the clear frame
passage as 105 × 65 mm and absence of fixed centre bars. Check eight actual
louver passages, overlap, drainage, rounded exposed edges and accessible screw
heads/tools. A matching journal diameter alone does not prove retention.

Use parameter assertions for dimensions and keepouts, plus bounded permitted-mode
CLI checks for representative invalid inputs (nonpositive dimensions/clearances,
collapsed louver passages, screw/hinge/catch conflicts) and one valid dimensional
variation. These are OpenSCAD assertion checks, not a unit-test suite. Measure the
complete printable envelope including louvers/hinge against the target printer
plate; record any slicer support/brim uncertainty. No slicer or physical print is
claimed. Actual unfused hinge operation, weather performance, connector fit and
mounting measurements remain user verification as approved in the SPEC.

## Definition of Done and blockers

All trace rows must have recorded evidence or their explicitly approved no-source
outcome. Complete review, corrections, required geometry QA, README and final
`git diff --check`; inspect staged content, commit accepted paths on main, verify
origin/main contains the commit and reconcile/clean the temporary worktree.
Physical fit and actual successful printing remain pending user checks and are
not claimed by completion of the CAD scope.

Missing approval/valid entry, failed base refresh, unsafe concurrent changes,
unavailable mandatory evidence, or geometry incompatible with required behavior
stops affected work as evidenced DRAFT after safe independent work. Native-agent
capacity is scheduling state: wait and resume pending assignments. Only true
missing information/authority/capability blocks; no routine continue question.
Preserve partial work and state exact unmet criteria, hashes and recovery paths.

### Final geometry approach and numeric allocation

One editable OpenSCAD2021.01 source, no dependencies. Add purpose-grouped named adjustable inputs for approved thicknesses/sections, preserving existing mounting inputs and supported modes/default printable_layout. Derived dimensions live separately; do not hide defaults in modules. No new render modes. All door features use the existing positive-X angle transform: installed +Y up, +Z outside, rear mounting planeZ0. Preserve outward/downward whole-panel operation, flat rear mounting face, two captive bed-supported bodies and no assembly.

The following default allocations are design intent to validate, not claimed render evidence:

- Keep opening105×65, core104.2×64.2, straight overlap3 and door envelope111×71. Panel3, frame3.6, overlap undersideZ4.0 and topZ7.0. Perimeter connection wall1.2 joins core to overlap with positive volumetric overlap. Louver roofs1.6 embed positively in core and retain eight open mouths; projection remains5, not5+extra-thickness. Four complete-depth head reliefs retain diameter9.8 at default clearance; extend their height to cover actual reinforced door geometry. Head cylinders now occupy Z3.6..7.6, diameter9, height4. Existing supported head-height domain0<height<=4 remains, with fresh full-sweep validation.
- Default axisY=-47.5,Z=5.5; journal radius1.8; head radius2.8. Arm X width2.4 with default startX30.1; bearing innerX34.1, axial width8; bridge undersideZ4.0 and thickness3 join the supported leg/journal. Printed arm/bearing X gap1.6. Journal throat allowance.4 per side and head axial gaps.4. Throat axial length3, head startX37.5, axial length2, chamberX37.1..39.9, remaining outer axial wall2.2. Head/toe radial sweep radius sqrt(5.5²+.4²)=5.5145; chamber radius=max(toe sweep,head radius)+.4=5.9145. Ensure throat retains the head/toe throughout motion, not merely diameter equality.
- Bearing upper chamber uses the full45-degree diamond profile. Its maximum profile-Y extent is sqrt(2)*chamber_radius, so choose exterior Y half-span sqrt(2)*chamber_radius+2=10.3645 (full20.729), rather than adding2 only to the lower circular half. Roof topZ=axisZ+sqrt(2)*chamber_radius+2=15.8645. Keep at least2mm outer side/roof material; intentional drainage opening and journal throats are exceptions. Outer bearing reaches Y=-37.1355, still outside aperture bottom-32.5, and overlaps the fixed lower border. Maintain through-underside drainage and no mandatory inaccessible interior support. Recalculate all envelopes for permitted parameter changes and reject unsupported combinations.
- Reallocate lug X start to arm_x+arm_width-.2=32.3, width1.4, ending33.7: .4 gap to bearing inner face34.1. It connects positively to journal material. Derive radial stop reach axisZ-fixed_stop_height=4.1; lug localY from axisY-4.1 to axisY-1.1, localZaxisZ..axisZ+1.4. Fixed stop spans X32.9..34.3 (moving supported-leg end32.5 plus.4 clearance) to overlap bearing by.2; lug contact X32.9..33.7 has width.8 and nominal90-degree area.8×1.4=1.12mm², YaxisY-1.6..axisY+.2 (depth1.8), Z0..1.4. Lug minimumZ=5.5-4.1sin(angle), strictly above1.4 until90 and matching at90; mirrored counterpart identical. Verify contact area and all intermediate separations after integration.

### Final geometric allocations

Use installed axes X horizontal, Y upward and Z outward. The rear mounting face and print bed are Z=0. All numbers below are millimetres unless marked degrees; adjustable dimensions retain _mm/_deg naming. The original plate125×85, screw110×70, shank4.5, head9×4, frame3.6, border10, door3, perimeter wall1.2, overlap3, louver projection5 and louver roof1.6 remain. Moving clearance g defaults to0.4.

**Hinge and bed support.** Set hinge axis Y=-54, Z=8, derive Y=-plate_height/2-11.5 and Z=max(8,frame_thickness+head_height+g). Smaller supported heads do not lower the axis. Recompute all axis-relative feet/housings/bounds consistently when a supported input raises the axis. Keep the supported screw-head-height domain0<height<=4; changing its domain requires new clearance allocation. Hinge span equals door width111, X=-55.5..55.5. Use11 segments with10 axial gaps g: segment length L=(111-10g)/11=9.727273, start_i=-55.5+i(L+g). Even indices0,2,4,6,8,10 belong to the door; odd indices1,3,5,7,9 belong to the frame. Segment count remains this selected odd count for this delivery; dimensional variations recompute L and reject collapsed lengths.

The continuous integral moving shaft is diameter5, joined to all six moving knuckles, never a loose pin. Moving collars have radius4.5. Use64 facets for circular hinge profiles. To preserve minimum radial clearance g despite faceting, implement fixed bore radius B=(shaft_radius+g)/cos(180/64), default2.903497. In local hinge coordinates u=Y-hinge_y and v=Z-hinge_z, form the lower semicircle and upper circular arc of radius B to45-degree tangent points (±B/sqrt(2),B/sqrt(2)), then straight roof slopes to(0,sqrt(2)*B). Define S=B+2, V=sqrt(2)*B+2*sqrt(2), J=V-S. The fixed exterior profile is(-S,-hinge_z),(S,-hinge_z),(S,J),(0,V),(-S,J). At defaults S=4.903497,J=2.031095,V=6.934593 and exterior topZ14.934593. These formulas, including the B adjustment, are the implemented allocation, giving2 mm side and normal roof thickness. The bore roof maximum radius4.106165 is smaller than the faceted collar's conservative inradius4.5*cos(180/64)=4.494580, leaving positive retention overlap approximately0.3884; the collar cannot pass axially through the bore. Each fixed segment is trapped between neighboring moving collars, with0.4 axial separation on both sides. Assert positive radial retention overlap and axial capture after every permitted parameter change.

For each moving segment, hull its radius4.5 collar with a forward-only rectangle u=0..3,v=-8..0. These integral feet directly contact the bed atZ0 without any negative-u bed toe. Frame housings contactZ0; fixed ribs within their own X bands reach Y=-42.3 atZ0..3.6, overlapping the fixed plate by0.2. Door bridges are2.4 wide in X atZ4..7, run fromY=-54 forward into the core by at least2 mm, and remain within moving-segment X bands. End bridge centres are X=±48.5, placing their outer X edges at±49.7, outside the expanded screw-head X interval starting at±50.1. Interior bridges are centred on their moving segments. Require positive volumetric unions, not tangent-only contacts.

Each shaft interval through a fixed segment bridges L+2g=10.527273 between bed-supported moving geometry. The fixed bore roofs are45 degrees; moving collar/foot hull rear lower tangents extend approximately3.72059 horizontally while rising5.46875. These are explicit bridge/overhang allocations, not proven slicer results. At the first door-bridge layer Z4, the forward collar/foot hull ends nearY=-50.206 and the core startsY=-31.727534, leaving approximately18.479 mm of unsupported2.4-wide bridge. Both ends must be connected printable anchors. This entire bridge must span support-free, including the part above the frame separated by only0.4; inspect its first layers and document the span. No internal support may be placed in bearings or moving gaps. Exterior supports remain permitted only with demonstrated accessible removal.

**Stops.** Provide two symmetric stop stations at moving segment2's left edge and moving segment8's right edge. Each occupies a2.4-wide X band wholly within that moving segment adjacent to fixed segment1 or9. In that band the moving lug is an annular sector r=3.5..7, polar angle0..10 degrees in(u,v), joined to the collar. The fixed stop is r=6..8, angle190..200 degrees, grown vertically downward toZ0. Connect it axially to the neighboring fixed housing through the same rear stop footprint; within the neighboring fixed band, join that footprint to the housing. Before180 the lug does not enter the fixed sector. Fixed downward supports remain at polar angles>=190; their nearest rear extent u<=-5.638 clears the radius4.5 collar. For a screw at Y=s, d=s-hinge_y, all inverse-transformed head points capable of meeting the panel in the first quadrant are inside an XY circle of radius d-sqrt((d-R)^2-hinge_z^2) about that screw, at most approximately5.282 upper and7.389 lower. Door motion uses the existing X-axis transform with the new axis. For every point, Z(theta)=8+u*sin(theta)+v*cos(theta). Forward hinge foot points(u>=0) have minimum bounded by endpoint heights. ### Assurance and validation

Select routine for this bounded local mechanical source/docs change: no external/shared software interface, deployment, executable dependency, security or credentials change. One independent integrated code-review wave is required. Contract/security reviews are inapplicable; physical hardware/motion interfaces are covered by geometry review and QA, not a software contract review. Independent QA is required because three captive bodies, inaccessible clearances,180-degree motion and gravity-latch capture need evidence beyond source review. Main performs documentation synchronization and final git diff --check. No unit tests or conventional test-first phase may be created or run.

All OpenSCAD invocations use timeout -k 1s 14s and write under /tmp/shed-ventilation-validation/A5/. Only printable_layout and assembly are allowed. Use an available offscreen/virtual display for PNG and actually view images. CSG generation is syntax/assertion evidence, not visual acceptance. STL/mesh inspection and temporary analytic scripts are geometry inspection, not repository unit tests. Never add generated exports or analysis scripts to source control. Example command forms:

```sh
timeout -k 1s 14s openscad -o /tmp/shed-ventilation-validation/A5/layout.csg -D 'render_mode="printable_layout"' designs/shed_ventilation.scad

git diff --check
```

Generate and inspect printable preview plus assembly door0/30/60/90/120/150/180 with retracted bolt, and closed locked/lifted/mid-slide/retracted latch poses. Add closeups of bore/shaft, both stops, guide capture, pockets, receiver and external support access. Continuous geometry bounds or equivalent complete motion analysis must cover the whole hinge sweep, all hardware envelopes, bolt travel plus permitted clearance play, and rear-plane nonpenetration. Verify shelf retention even while lifted, no premature180stop collision, no passage obstruction at90/180 and genuine bolt capture with the vent off the wall. A three-component STL count alone cannot establish captivity.

Mesh checks must establish exactly three finite closed connected components, each with actual stable Z0 contact and printable connected growth. Confirm all as-printed moving gaps, roofs/bridges, material unions, positive locked and retracted pockets, capture shoulders, eight passages and screw/tool access. Inspect the selected external supports' removal path; never substitute inaccessible support for bed-supported captive geometry. Report final bounds/plate margins. Source-derived geometry and preview evidence do not prove slicer output or unfused printing; physical checks remain as approved.

Run a valid dimensional variation plate_width135/screw_spacing_x120 and recompute span/segment/plate bounds. Check negative guards for nonpositive dimensions/gaps, collapsed segments or vents, lost hinge/bolt capture, wall penetration, invalid pose, unsupported head height, screw/hinge/receiver conflicts and lost stop engagement. Inspect OpenSCAD diagnostic output rather than exit code alone. Record precise commands, outcomes, source hashes and artifact paths. Timeouts remain missing evidence; no render over15 seconds, repeated successful unchanged checks or invented evidence. Parent consolidates supported review findings, commissions one bounded correction batch, then affected checks and QA. Repeat a cycle only for confirmed acceptance failure, regression or blocked approved behavior, with benefit exceeding impact. In-scope corrections continue without routine permission. Newly discovered geometry requiring an approved-behavior change follows the active implementation amendment rules after valid implement entry; this user-invoked planning turn retains manual SPEC/PLAN gates. ### Complete default proof allocation

Let c be flange centre; R its rigid rotation; n=R[:,z]; tX=nX/nZ and tY=nY/nZ. Start at the nominal print/operation pose and consider the FIRST departure from total inclination beta<=7 degrees, centre3<=cZ<=4.8 and the connected yaw branch containing zero. In this enclosing region, flange vertical half-extent is bounded by1.5*cos(beta)+sqrt(128)*sin(beta)<2.868. Its entire Z extent is strictly inside the sidewall height0..8.2, so actual sidewalls constrain its XY projection. Its inscribed radius8 disc yields52.540<=cY<=56.460 and similarly constrained cX. Four vertical flange sections at global offsets(±3.5,±6.5) lie inside the flange and outside both roof and rear apertures. Finite-thickness containment uses sqrt(3.5²+6.5²)/cos(7)+1.5*tan(7)<7.624<8. Their Ys are strictly below50.1 or above57.9 while remaining within44.6..64.4, and their Xs remain inside the chamber. Both roof and bottom retaining surfaces therefore oppose every section.

Necessary confinement is7*abs(tX)+13*abs(tY)+3/nZ<=3.8. At beta7 the left side is at least7*tan7+3*sec7>3.8, excluding the first inclination boundary. Symmetric sections force3.5<=cZ<=4.3, excluding both enclosing centre boundaries. The flange cannot reach the first ±45-degree yaw boundary because its Y projection would exceed the19.8 chamber depth. These facts close the reachable envelope; in particular c cannot cross the rear or roof plane, and closed sidewalls prevent lateral escape. This is an orientation/translation capture proof independent of pad sidedness or continued stem engagement, not a sampled-pose claim. ## Amendment 8 — 2026-10-16 — quarter-turn latch and reinforced support-free hoods

- Amendment ID: PLAN-shed-ventilation-A8
- Previous version: complete effective PLAN through A7 and its execution receipt. Preserve all earlier bytes and document status.
- Approval: Approved by user on 2026-10-16
- Approved SPEC: complete effective SPEC through SPEC-shed-ventilation-A8, explicitly approved by user reply “approve” on 2026-10-16; SHA-256 6efadadeaa6b89ed5af81d8cca80a51a5bfde5f056010f1c5a404c3d1042d63a.
- Request and purpose: replace the lift/slide closure with the selected quarter-turn mechanism and remodel the eight blades without removable blade supports. This amendment supersedes old bolt allocations and execution units only where named below. It grants no implementation authority before separate PLAN approval and valid implement entry.

### Changes against the previous version and final-state allocation

The researched baseline is invoking main at 089c6be0fec640ce1e979d896c8e98a6ec89fb28; designs/shed_ventilation.scad SHA-256 3c68000005d8898eeada4f2e080d897f91ac5690cbaa51357bf8e8fc14ac46b9. Use the existing vent_slots, door_louvers, moving_door, fixed_frame and vent_assembly source patterns. Do not reopen unrelated designs or previously settled mounting/hinge choices. All dimensions below are manually selected design allocations, not measured hardware or empirical print limits.

| Feature | Before | Final implementation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Closure | A6/A7 lift 3 / slide 16 mm bolt, guide, receiver and bolt-specific assertions | Axis-Z captive quarter-turn rotor with gravity-biased end stops; remove the old bolt, guide, slide/lift inputs and their obsolete proofs. Preserve/renew the small door receiver as allocated below. |
| Hoods | 5 mm projection, upper-edge-only roofs | 9 mm projecting roofs with steeper undersides, side cheeks and two permanent ribs per hood; eight hood groups, each subdivided into three channels. |
| Printing | Rear-down, closed/retracted/seated, nine islands | Rear-down closed door and central rotor print angle 0 degrees, clear of both end stops; rotor operating angles -45 released / +45 locked. Three captive bodies; recompute actual islands. |
| Documentation | Lift-slide operation and old support coordinates | Quarter-turn operation, gravity orientation/parking, reinforcement dimensions, support-free blade growth, accessible rotor support where required, and new evidence/limits. |

#### B1: hood geometry allocation

Keep source-derived slot centres, widths and heights and the current eight slots. Let s=slot_height_mm, u=s/2+1, l=-s/2, k=7.6/(s+1). In the existing local roof yz coordinates, relative to the door face, replace the quadrilateral with [(u,-0.2),(u,1.4),(l,9),(l,7.4)]. The lower roof surface is -0.2+(u-y)*k and upper surface is 1.4+(u-y)*k. This is a 1.6 mm vertical section, not a claim of 1.6 mm normal thickness. At defaults the underside is approximately 45.904 degrees; it remains above 45 degrees over the preserved clearance domain. Assert that slope floor rather than silently accepting unsupported dimension combinations. The roof ends remain separated from adjacent rows by 6 mm in Y.

Each hood receives two 1 mm-wide side cheeks just outside its slot X boundaries and two 1.2 mm-wide internal ribs centred at local X=±slot_width_mm/6. Cheeks occupy [slot_width/2,slot_width/2+1] and its negative mirror; their positive overlap with the roof's 1 mm lateral extensions is intentional. Cheek/rib footprints span local Y=[l-1,u], starting at global Z=0. Within Y=[l,u], their top is global door_thickness_mm + lower_roof_surface(y) + 0.4, below the upper roof surface and overlapping the roof by 0.4 mm. In the extra lower root strip [l-1,l], their top is door_thickness_mm; this strip anchors into the lower door web without extending the high roof beyond its defined mouth. Upper/lower root overlaps are 1 mm, subject to actual rounded-slot intersection checks; require positive-volume root/roof unions and increase only local root overlap if rounding reduces the allocated union, without closing airflow channels.

The ribs begin on the bed and join door webs at both ends, rather than starting as unsupported roof attachments. Their tops rise from existing solid material; the roof grows from its upper anchored edge. Preserve the real downward outlets between ribs. Report each channel's minimum section and actual through-air path separately from nominal slot plan area. At defaults the two ribs remove 2.4 mm from each 43.1 mm-wide slot, yielding about 2072.04 mm² total remaining slot plan area across eight groups before other restrictions; this is not an airflow rating. Record actual mesh/open-area results, including side cheeks and roots.

#### B2: quarter-turn geometry allocation

Use a pivot perpendicular to the mounting plane, at X=-12, Y=53 mm. Rotor Z is unchanged by rotation, removing the axis-Y candidate's below-wall swept-foot problem. Source pose input latch_angle_deg uses -45 released, +45 locked and 0 for printable_layout. Assembly open-door poses require the released angle. This layout retains three permanent bodies and no assembly. All local XY values below are relative to the pivot; Z values are global.

Rotor: cylindrical bed foot radius 6, Z0..2.8 (113.097 mm² footprint, 12 mm diameter); integral flange radius 8, Z2.4..5.4; spindle radius 3, Z5..17.5. The foot/flange overlap is 0.4 mm, spindle/flange overlap 0.4 mm. Fixed housing outer radius 10.8 with cavity radius 8+g, lower retaining lip Z0..2 with opening radius 6+g, and roof Z5.4+g..7.8+g with spindle opening radius 3+g, where g is moving_clearance_mm. Connect the housing to the frame with an anchored foot at Z0..frame_thickness_mm above the door receiver's swept Y extent, preserving at least g actual clearance. The default housing minimum Y is 42.2; the existing receiver maximum Y is 41.6. Keep the full opening unobstructed. Do not fill the rotor's lower opening or moving cavity with frame anchor material.

The retaining flange exceeds both front and rear openings. Renew an all-reachable-play confinement proof using the finite cavity height/radius, flange thickness, openings and spindle; nominal radii alone are insufficient. Expected total axial freedom is 0.4+g, at most 0.8 mm. Bound inclination and lateral translation conservatively, and apply the same envelope to capture, blocker engagement, roof clearance and end stops. No retention argument may rely on the wall or a forced snap. Carry forward the explicit SPEC A6 distinction: installed wall contact clips inward normal play of the named rear-contact geometry, while off-wall capture is independently established. Name the rotor foot and adjoining low-Z flange material as the new latch rear-contact exceptions. Nominal axis-Z travel leaves their Z unchanged. Bound the full off-wall axial/tilt play, but do not claim an absent wall clamps it above Z0. Separately report installed wall-clipped play and rear rubbing/rough-siding limitations, without changing installation or adding wall spacers.

Rigid blocker bar: local X[-1.5,1.5], Y[-19,0], Z10.5..13.5, joined to the spindle through a positive-volume hub. Door receiver retains X[-5,5], Y33..41.6 and top Z8.6, with its existing connected root; remove any obsolete receiver feature not needed for this engagement. At +45 the blocker crosses the receiver; at -45 it is laterally clear. Default locked nominal underside gap is 1.9 mm, allowing finite print play while still catching outward door movement. Prove actual retained motion before the overlap can be lost, including initial door swing and worst reachable rotor play. Check external finger access with the door shut. No forced closure against the blocker is permitted: release before opening or closing, then turn to lock.

Counterweight/thumb tab: local X[-5,5], Y[6,22], Z15..21, connected by a rigid web local X[-1.5,1.5], Y[0,6.4], Z15..21 to the spindle; its 0.4 mm overlap into the tab is intentional. It deliberately puts the rotor centre of mass above the pivot when installed. Its rectangular volume is 960 mm³, with upward first moment 13440 mm⁴; the blocker bar is 171 mm³ with downward moment 1624.5 mm⁴. Verify height-clipped and complete rotor COM remains inside its own radius6 bed support polygon before any substantial connection/support; record margin and any accessible weight support. Recompute the complete rotor's actual mass moment including foot/flange/hub/web/stop lug and verify gravity torque points toward the corresponding end stop throughout each half of the operating arc, with a single unstable central crossover. Both parked states must have restoring gravity torque against their stops. This is gravity-biased positive mechanical parking, not friction-only retention, a flex snap, a lock or a wind-load rating. The installed +Y-up orientation is necessary and must be documented.

Add a rotor stop lug in the annular band radius10..13 at polar angles85..95 degrees, Z10..13, with a rigid root web local X[-1.5,1.5], Y[0,7.4], Z10..13 connected to the spindle and an annular-sector neck radius7..13 at the same85..95-degree polar angles. The web stays inside radius7.551, clear of the fixed stops beginning at radius9; only the narrow sector neck/lug establishes the endpoint contacts. Fixed stop sectors occupy radii9..14, polar angles30..40 and140..150, Z0..13; connect them positively to the housing over the 1.8 mm radial overlap and start them on the bed; the inner radius9 clears the maximum cavity8.4 by0.6 mm. They must not enter rotor sweep except at deliberate end contacts. Polar angles are measured from +X. Nominal contact occurs at rotor -45/+45. Free clearance around the centre print angle avoids fused print contacts. Include actual radial/axial play and resulting endpoint angle variation in the capture, parking and release/engagement envelopes; do not treat ideal stop angles as zero-play proof. Axis-Z rotation keeps the rotor above the rear plane even with angular endpoint variation.

Print-growth limitations: the flange overhangs the foot by 2 mm radially and the fixed roof reaches inward toward the spindle opening. Both begin from existing printed material, but their overhang/bridge quality is unproven. The blocker and counterweight may require accessible exterior support above the housing/frame/door; define its exact final coordinates and lateral removal paths from the final geometry, keeping it clear of blades, moving gaps and internal cavities. Do not use internal supports or fuse the captive parts. The blade support-free requirement does not prohibit accessible support on the new latch. If the assigned geometry cannot meet a preserved modeled criterion, use the implement amendment path before changing allocation; never weaken a guard to pass.

### Planning assurance and approval record

On 2026-10-16 independent explorer inspected the repository, source inventory, approved SPEC, complete A8 draft, trace, validation and delivery assumptions. Main disposed these findings by shortening the broad web and using a sector neck, enlarging the foot to radius6 with opening6+g and renewed capture obligations, raising spindle to17.5 and tab/web to15..21, and using bed-starting fixed stops through13. Both explorer and architect then independently inspected the corrected geometry paragraphs, validation and unchanged SPEC against PLAN SHA-25647e630db7adc2b6c8a4f38457b9c3037689e3fb655e97d9ec2a7c64d47880e8e and SPEC SHA-2566efadadeaa6b89ed5af81d8cca80a51a5bfde5f056010f1c5a404c3d1042d63a. ### Selected geometry and interface

Installed coordinates remain +Y up, +Z outside, bed/wall Z0. All dimensions below are manually selected provisional PETG design inputs; expose adjustable values by purpose and keep derived coordinates separate. Physical calibration is unproven.

Ear spans X[-18,-13] and [13,18], carrier hub X[-12.6,12.6] (0.4 side gaps), hub OD10. Ears have >=3 mm radial wall and bed-starting webs. Keep stationary mount above actual catch/door sweep with >=0.4 mm gap. This taller mount deliberately increases the complete vent height to approximately161 mm; recompute full bounds and printer fit. Screws remain at X±55 and existing holes unchanged.
2. Carrier body X[-6,6],Y[34,60.4],Z[12.4,15.4]. Sideways-flexing beam X[9.4,11.4],Y[36,60.4],Z[9,15.4], with free flex length24 along Y, bending thickness2 along X, depth6.4 along Z. Root bridge X[4,11.4],Y[60,64],Z[12.4,15.4] ensures0.4 positive-volume root overlap; connect to hub through >=3 mm solid sections. Root fillets >=2 mm. Distal toe X[8.4,11.4],Y[35,38],Z[7.5,9.6] joins beam by0.6 in Z. Provide >=8×8 mm accessible finger pad, positioned so its full movement remains clear. These are selected dimensions, not proof of PETG flex life.
3. Door catch lip X[3,9],Y[35,38],Z[10,12], rooted from X[2,5] to existing top receiver/perimeter with >=3 mm root sections and >=2 mm fillets. Preserve the current receiver root at X±5,Y33..41.6; inspect any extended lip overhang and adjacent hood clearance. Toe rests behind lip with0.4 Z gap and0.6 X overlap. A45-degree lead-in over >=1 mm guides engagement. Press beam +X by a derived release displacement: minimum1.0mm nominal, increased by the complete adverse axial translation and pin/hub tilt envelope at the toe, with maximum permitted tip travel4.0mm. Require>=0.4mm actual lip clearance throughout that full reachable play envelope; neither1.0 nor1.4mm is a universal release guarantee. If required travel exceeds4.0mm, amend the interface before implementation proceeds, keeping it displaced until toe clears in outward carrier swing (inspect first15degrees and solve exact release angle). Add lower-Y retaining wall X[3,6],Y[33,34.6],Z[9.6,15.4], positively united with carrierY>=34/Z>=12.4; catch beginsY35, leaving0.4 gap. This wall prevents the catch slipping toward-Y while door and carrier rotate together. Verify the released wall clears above the lip before Y gaps close. The lip blocks outward-Z door motion, not friction alone. Explicitly analyze coupled reachable door/carrier motion: independent sweeps alone do not prove locked retention.
4. Positive released parking uses a second fixed lip for the same tongue at carrier180degrees: X[7,9],Y[90,93],Z[12.9,14.9], with toe transformed to X[8.4,11.4],Y[90,93],Z[10.4,12.5], yielding0.4 Z gap and0.6 X overlap. Connect lip via X[7,9],Y[92.6,94],Z[12.9,14.9] to rear support X[7,10],Y[93.6,97],Z[0,14.9]. The rigid carrier ends atX6, giving1mm lateral clearance to lip/support; toe maximum sweptY is approximately93.108, giving>=0.492mm clearance to rear supportY93.6. Trim root fillets against swept keepouts rather than filling those gaps; same derived +X release and4.0mm maximum travel. Inspect full0..180 carrier sweep including intentional endpoint engagement. This provides stable parking without assuming gravity holds an upright clasp. The parked assembly must clear the complete door0..180 sweep.
5. Independent PETG pin diameter5, bore circular envelope5.8 (0.4 radial allowance), head diameter10/thickness2. The36mm bearing span is X[-18,18], not the full shaft length. Head occupies X[-20,-18], shaft X[-18,22] (40mm), groove X[18.4,20.8] (2.4mm wide), with intact end shoulder X[20.8,22]. A2mm clip can seat with its inner face X18.4..18.8, giving0.4..0.8 axial play relative to the far earX18. Groove core diameter4. Separate PETG C-clip thickness2, overall width>=12, two rounded arms>=2 wide with>=12 flexible length, groove engagement0.5 radial. Derive clip opening/throat from4mm core and0.5 overlap, with running clearance outside engaged shoulders. Show finite retaining contact, clip insertion/removal trajectory and no spontaneous pin extraction through any reachable clasp pose; no asserted material strength. Enlarged head and clip stay outside stationary ears; no shed-wall access needed.
6. Print clasp carrier flat with flex bending in its layer plane, clip flat, and pin shaft on a derived D-flat>=2 mm wide with a matching head flat. Keep the pin's bearing envelope within its nominal5mm circle. Each component has stable bed contact and an independently selectable printable_layout arrangement. Use bed-supported peaked/teardrop ear roofs enclosing the5.8mm bore envelope, no removable support in bores/hook contacts. Inspect actual hook, parking-catch and hub growth; use localized self-supporting root gussets outside mating/swept keepouts, without changing specified interfaces. No sacrificial fusions. Layout has>=8mm part spacing and all parts on bed; show exactly five permanent material bodies: frame, captive door, separate clasp, pin, clip. Assembly controls show locked, release-displaced and positively parked states; no additional render modes. Preserve default printable_layout.

Every interface is constrained by engagement, release and clearance above; exact derived carrier transitions and polygon tessellation are reversible implementation choices, not authority to change these dimensions or behavior. ### Selected interface allocation and provisional manual dimensions

Coordinates below are in the current closed assembly, millimetres. All new values are manually selected provisional PETG inputs, not measured fits or established strength. For the wide variant add `catch_x_offset_mm=(plate_width_mm-125)/2` to every closure X coordinate. Keep generic hinge clearance `0<g<=0.4`; cartridge fits and jaw interference have their own named parameters and must not collapse with `g`.

1. **Stud and capture:** stud axis parallel Z, spherical head diameter4.4, centre `(58,8,8)`, stem diameter2.8 and root fillet radius0.8. Use a true rounded head rather than a sharp retaining barb. The relaxed jaw throat is3.0 wide, centredX58; a centred head needs0.8 spread per jaw for4.6 throat width. Allocate independent jaw spreads0..2.1 to accommodate the full screening offset budget1.25 plus0.1 passage clearance per side; see the finite-play allocation below. Lowest retaining lip planeZ9.8 is above the head equator; at that plane the sphere's nominal cross-section is about2.53 wide, leaving about0.235 clearance per side. The head is geometrically captured on outward pull but not held in continuous peak-spread preload. Verify actual faceted surfaces, socket play and contacts rather than treating these section numbers as a proof.
2. **Jaw arms:** two parallel cantilevers along Y, free unbraced structure from rootY-16 to tipY8, comprising20mm uniform section throughY4 and the shaped compliant tip thereafter, bending outward in ±X. Relaxed beam boxesX55.7..57.1 and58.9..60.3; section1.4 in X by2.0 in Z, Z9.8..11.8. Use radius1.0 root transitions behind the free-span start, rounded entry/exit lips reachingX56.5 and59.5 and no tip material beyondY10.0. Spread poses must rotate local sections with their beam tangent; do not stretch rigid tip blocks arbitrarily. Scallop the tip stock overY4..10 to expose the throat surfaces and relaxed head clearance, retaining at least0.8mm local material thickness. Peak envelopeX53.6..62.4 stays on the border. Use a variable-section beam screening calculation with the actual shaped tip/root; the old uniform24mm strain estimate is retired and must not be presented as evidence for this section. Tip stress, layer strength, creep and material response remain unproved.
3. **Stud attachment and pull access:** a simple transverse bridge below the head is prohibited because it can strike a jaw during release. Route the stem into a central longitudinal tongueX56.6..59.4, Y8..14, with supportZ4.4..6.0; make a broad cross-bridge from the door's retained side web to that tongue overY12..16. Use positive overlapping unions and radius0.8 transitions, keeping the head/stem continuous. Tips must end byY10 so the bridge leads them during the initial curved withdrawal. Local door overlap next to the arms/root is trimmed toX52.5 overY-29..10 (zero nominal overlap only within this closure interval); the existing core edgeX52.1 atg0.4, and its supported-gap limit approaching52.5, remain intact; do not cut the door core or louver roots. Replace the old press recess locally with a raised pull ledge overY18..26, X53.0..55.0, undersideZ8 and topZ10; connect through a broad inner webX51.0..53.0. Clear the old material below the2mm X purchase, exposing a side opening from+X above frameZ3.6 (4.4mm nominal height). Round edgesR0.8. This is geometric finger purchase, not proof of comfortable access; it must be reachable without inserting a finger through the cartridge. Verify finger purchase for outward pull and the finite curved escape of tongue/bridge under relevant hinge play.
4. **Cartridge root and service:** connect the two arms by a broad rigid cross-root behindY-16. Allocate rootX55.0..61.6, Y-28..-16, Z9.8..11.8, coplanar with the arms; remove the earlier proposed raised-arm risers. No transverse rigid brace may shorten either unbraced jaw region. Frame-mounted keyed rails provide positive retention against outward and inward closure loads, not friction alone. Select two interrupted roof stations overY-27..-25 andY-21..-19, paired with discrete1mm-thick side-flange tabs atY-26.8..-25.2 andY-20.8..-19.2. Slide+Y3 to place those flanges in the clear gaps before lifting. Root side flanges are1.2mm wide, roof overlap at least0.8mm, lateral running allowance0.03mm per side at the dedicated guide lands and vertical allowance0.2mm. Root undersideZ9.8 rests above floorZ9.6. Lower every material region under roof footprints toZ10.8 maximum, leaving roof undersideZ11 and topZ12; do not retain a full-height crossbar under a roof. Fixed inner postX53.6..54.85 and outer postX61.75..62.4 support roofs ending atX55.8 and60.8 respectively. These posts remain outside the root; add dedicated lateral guide lands atY-27.8 andY-18.4 on the central root faces, not on the disappearing flange tabs, with9.4mm station separation and0.04mm per-side clearance. Do not confuse the looser post-to-flange gap with guide play. Central root isX56.2..60.4; flange bands areX55.0..56.2 and60.4..61.6. Omit flange-band material completely except the two specified tabs: there must be no continuous lowered flange. At withdrawal+3 the roofs map to cartridge-localY-30..-28 andY-24..-22; full-depth windows extend toY-27.8 at the rear andY-24.2..-21.8 at the middle, clear of tabs. Lower any high root stock beneath the swept roof projections over the entire3mm translation, not just the seated roof footprints. Verify actual guide lands are0.4mm long in Y with contact heightZ10.9..11.8, supported from side posts outside central-rootX56.2..60.4. They clear tabs of height<=10.8 during sliding. At withdrawal+3 the front tab startsY-17.8, leaving0.4mm to the front guide endY-18.2. Verify the rear tabs also clear their guide and the central root lifts without an overhanging capture feature. Use1.2mm local downward service-arm relief beneath the pad; no belowZ0 intrusion. A rear hard stop takes-Y loads. A retained integral service arm in the root has10mm actual free span,0.8mm bending thickness,1.2mm breadth, R0.6 root, square0.8mm retaining shoulder and4×4mm finger pad; deliberate1mm depression clears the +Y retaining stop. With the door180 degrees open, depress the pad, slide+Y3, relax it, then lift+Z until the root clears the roof. Reverse for installation. Floor/roof retention must carry normal snap loads without loading the service arm in its release direction. Fixed socket posts/roof and arm/pad must fitX53.6..62.4,Y-28.4..-15.6,Z3.6..12 and preserve the nominal passage. Validate actual finite roof/rail/shoulder stock, lateral and tilt play and clear lift trajectory, not nominal section overlap alone; incompatibility blocks rather than silently weakening retention. They remain wholly on the right mounting border, never in the nominal open passage.
5. **Print layout:** keep the frame/door rear-down and the cartridge flat with common root/arm undersideZ9.8 translated to the bed, with its broad stable face down and no new floating root or arm. Arrange the cartridge beside the vent with at least8mm separation, total oriented cartridge X span at most10mm; full layout must fit the143/153×85×12 limits. Account for relaxed lips, root/keys and all actual material. The assembled depth stays at most12mm. Maintain selectors `all`, `vent`, `catch`; `catch` now selects the jaw cartridge. Assembly inspection exposes independent left/right jaw spread0..2.1 and the distinct service coordinates; invalid simultaneous normal/service states assert. Printable layout ignores all assembly pose controls.

Bore halfplanes bound shaft-centre Y offsets by±0.40362 and Z by-0.40362..+0.57081. These allocations are a concrete design to validate, not proof that simultaneous constraints are satisfied. Do not silently alter hinge, plate, passage or louver allocation to make them fit. Discovery of incompatibility uses the applicable amendment path before affected source changes.

### Units, assurance and serialized execution

Select **routine** assurance: bounded local CAD mechanism change with no software API, external producer/consumer interface, executable dependency, deployment, credentials, permissions or security change. Contract/security reviews are inapplicable for those reasons. Mechanical interfaces are explicitly covered by integrated review and functional QA. Distinct QA is required because curved snap engagement, cartridge retention and independent first-layer growth need evidence beyond source review. Unit tests and conventional test-first phases are prohibited.

| J6 | Parent: final four-path reconciliation, local main/origin/main | J5, final DoD | Accepted staging only, diff check, verified main commit and exact remote destination SHA; guarded cleanup |

Every native assignment is fresh-context, bounded to five minutes with interruption, verified artifact/source digests and exact immutable ownership; parent reconciles uncertain work before a successor. Use at most available capacity; serialize source mutations, artifact changes and repository checks. Temporary capacity shortage queues work without ending execution. Children never commit/push/deploy/manage worktrees. Ordinary checkpoints are intermediate; continue through remaining units without a routine continuation question. Reversible defaults are helper names, facet counts adequate for clearance evidence, temporary filenames and equivalent diagnostic formatting; they may not change the selected interface or behavior.

### Validation and evidence boundary

Development validates default/wide widths atg0.4 andg0.01 (the latter CAD regression only), supported continuous gap guards, and named invalid inputs. Use bounded commands such as `timeout -k 1s 13s openscad -o /tmp/shed-jaws/layout.csg -D 'render_mode="printable_layout"' designs/shed_ventilation.scad` and the corresponding assembly command with `door_angle_deg=180`; only permitted modes, no electronics. For STL/preview inspection use the same per-command cap and temporary outputs. Inspect diagnostics even on exit0; stop any background render before15 seconds. Do not add generated artifacts or unit tests.

For engagement, solve the actual hinge transform against rounded lip/head contact, with a continuous or conservatively bounded path from seated through peak spread to released and reverse closing. Account for local tangent rotation, finite faceting, relevant axial/radial hinge play, cartridge/socket play and the stud support bridge; sampled endpoint pictures alone do not establish traversal. Inspect all other material for noncontact throughout0..180, preserve hinge capture/stops, maintain positive as-printed frame/door gaps and no operation behindZ0. Prior hinge proofs may be reused only after independently verifying source/requirements/dependencies unchanged; closure interactions and newly changed door material require new coverage.

Determine finite watertight body components, actual closed/layout bounding boxes and each Z0 island from current geometry, not README values. Report footprint shape, area, narrow dimensions and necks, height of first substantial union, sampled initial growth and each independent island's support polygon until union. Assess support/bridge access, rounded stud growth, jaw-root print orientation and contact-face quality. Keep existing sole floors where applicable; do not fuse clearances with support or indiscriminate brim. QA must distinguish finite samples/analytical bounds from physically unproved deposition.

Run `git diff --check` before presentation and delivery. Slicer toolpath/first-layer review and physical fit, adhesion, one-handed opening, automatic closing, retention, jaw recovery, fatigue and environment/cable/thermal outcomes remain pending without hardware evidence. This is a modeled-delivery scope inherited from A13, not an instruction to fabricate physical success or operate a printer. README must state these omitted evidence categories; no claim that breakage is physically fixed.

## Amendment 32 — 2026-10-19 — robust side closure and upward top hinge

- Amendment ID: PLAN-shed-ventilation-A32
- Previous version: complete effective PLAN through A31, delivered at `48f78cf808ee28c3c361f55756ed68078b884e20`.
- Approval: Pending required independent plan assurance, then automatic approval under the originating `$implement specs/PLAN-shed-ventilation.md` request.
- Paired authority: complete explicitly user-approved SPEC through A18, including A16/A17; approval-recorded SPEC SHA-256 `2f5d72255e0ecc85fa7bf7cd4c5c4381b1c1f95fb32bc138721e1c102f9f8948`.
- Request and purpose: strengthen both closure parts, replace the round male with a chamfered rectangular lug, move the whole door hinge to the top, retain 180-degree travel and provide clip-retained seating plus gravity return after the initial opening-biased interval. The user's latest `approve` approves A18. Internal routing to iterate was erroneous: no user invoked iterate, so required amendments remain within active implement. Preserve all original text/status and approval history; no further routine manual gate follows this amendment's assurance and automatic approval.

### Changes against the previous version

| Allocation | A31 | A32 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Closed catch | Elevated 2 × 1.4 mm leaf and spherical male | Bed-grown tall side leaf with isolated free span; chamfered rectangular male on a broad carrier |
| Door motion | Bottom X axis, positive rotation | Top X axis Y38.5/Z6.5 mm, outward/upward negative X rotation, 0–180 degrees |
| Open retention | Bottom resting stop | Independent inward-flexing frame leaf and concave door cam at the top, preloaded against the integral 180-degree stop |
| Evidence | Delivered bottom-hinge contact/printing certificates | Fresh contact, captivity, gravity, open-hold, sweep, first-growth and support-removal evidence; old certificates are algorithms/reference only |

### Final geometry allocation and preserved interfaces

All numbers below are manually selected provisional CAD allocations, not measured PETG calibrations. Preserve A16/A17's exact plate, mounting, passage, louver, nominal closed/print-layout and rear-plane boundaries, two operational PETG bodies and stable rear-down no-installation printing. Keep closed louvers facing down: transform hinge features only. Preserve OpenSCAD 2021.01, one SCAD, no libraries, source default `printable_layout`, explicit assembly inspection, and P2S/AMS 2 Pro targets. A complete revised reprint is allowed. No unrelated design or printer operation is owned.

**Closed catch.** Retain the side location and existing bridge beyond the free leaf end instead of inventing a long bottom carrier. In narrow-width coordinates, use a leaf body X55.3..56.3, Y−20..8.2, Z0..12 mm: 28.2 mm free length, 1 mm bending thickness and 12 mm breadth, bending toward −X. Keep a broad root Y−27..−20 with at least R1.2 shoulders and positive frame union. Isolate the free leaf with a frame slot X52.5..56.7, Y−20..8.7 through the frame; the outer frame bar remains 5.8 mm wide. Shift this entire side allocation by the established +5 mm wide-variant offset. Retain/extend the local moving right-lip relief; do not cut the central opening or move mounting holes. Inspect 0..2 mm maximum provisional leaf flex and whole neighboring stock, including finite hinge play.

The retaining nose has a supported 45-degree underside from X56.3/Z9.4 to X57.5/Z10.6 and top Z12. The male's nominal envelope is X56.7..59.7, Y6..10, Z4.4..10.2; its upper inner edge has a 0.8 mm X/Z chamfer, leaving a nominal 0.4 mm gap beneath that nose. Ease both Y-direction entry/exit faces and edges; use a broad root/carrier at Y10..14 and the existing transverse bridge near Y14 beyond the leaf end. Increase the former narrow spine to at least 3 mm width, with positive-volume unions and eased load transitions. A ramped underside can rise from Y10/Z4.4 toward Y6/Z8.4, leaving at least 1.8 mm stock at the forward contact end. This is a chamfered rectangular paddle, not a spherical head. Closed relaxed body-to-body separation is at least the selected running gap. Establish actual capture and continuous reversible cam deflection from the replacement finite geometry; these dimensions alone are not a contact certificate. The 2 mm flex is an inspection ceiling, not a required or physically established travel.

The tall leaf's approximate rectangular stiffness proxy is proportional to 12×1³ versus the old 1.4×2³, while its section area grows from 2.8 to 12 mm². This is a handling/cleanup allocation with similar idealized lateral stiffness, not a force or strength claim. Recalculate for the actual nose, fillets, section and deformed shape. Do not fill the clearance slot or fuse the free span to the frame.

**Top hinge/capture.** Retain the existing nine-band span formula H=plate_width−26 and L=(H−8g)/9 as placement references, not unchanged geometry certificates. Mirror the hinge profile/feet/stops in Y, set the new axis Y38.5/Z6.5, rederive the door-core radial guard and screw relief, and rotate the complete moving door by −angle. Keep the left fixed band0/moving band1, followed by a fixed terminal bearing from end(band1)+g to X−24; left shaft ends there. Remove original central bands2–4 and their continuous shaft. On the right use the former band4 only from Xg to its original right end, followed by moving5/fixed6/moving7/fixed8 and the right shaft. Thus no shaft or housing crosses the central leaf/root corridor X−23..0. At g0.4 the narrow terminal bearing is approximately 3.41 mm long and the trimmed right bearing 4.92 mm; verify all supported widths/gaps. Moving collars must bracket actual bearing ends with positive capture under combined translation, rotation, axial and tilt play, not just a nominal radius comparison. Start from shaft radius1.3, collar radius2.8, bearing wall1.5 and the existing self-supporting bore; amend allocations before acceptance if capture requires changes. Keep/reinforce moving sole floors of at least60 mm² and minimum4 mm short dimension after all stop reliefs; reallocate roots/bridges locally as necessary. Re-establish 0/180 hard stops and head/tool keepouts over the whole sweep.

**Integral open hold.** Use a separate frame-integral bed-grown leaf, free X−18..0 and root X−23..−18 with R1.5 shoulders; body Y38.9..40.9/Z0..8, 18 mm free length, 2 mm bending thickness, 8 mm breadth. It bends −Y into a channel offering 1.2 mm flex plus running clearance, isolated from fixed stock. An outward supported 45-degree tip ramp runs from Y40.9/Z6.4 to Y42.5/Z8. Place a door-integral concave annular cam in X−3..0, broadly rooted to the upper door lip/carrier. In the fully open pose its outer radius is5.5 and angular sector β0..27 degrees, measured from+Y toward+Z; truncate31 degrees to27 so the closed low end starts above Z4.0 at the default gap. Ease inner-profile joins through β0.56/R4.67, β12.56/R3.80, β20.56/R4.18 mm. Nominal tip radius4.272 atβ20.56 gives approximately0.51 mm peak and0.10 mm seated inward flex. This is a concave inner cam pushing inward, with preload toward the 180 hard stop. Model actual finite ramp/cam sections, not point contact. Local frame pocketing beneath the cam may provide closed clearance without reducing the passage. No new floating cam start or trapped support is acceptable.

Use provisional homogeneous-solid density1.27 mg/mm³ and elastic modulus1500 N/mm² only as explicit calculation assumptions; include modulus sensitivity and report the minimum modeled modulus for hold stability. The rectangular open-leaf estimate k≈4.12 N/mm and seat-to-peak elastic rise≈0.51 N·mm are feasibility screens only. Establish positive seated restoring moment AND a continuous combined elastic/gravitational escape barrier with the actual replacement mass/COM, hinge play and leaf/cam deformation. A peak-force comparison or isolated spring-energy difference is insufficient. Physical creep, friction, infill and stay-open behavior remain pending. The door must deliberately leave this hold by finger movement, without cable/tool prop or third body. If numeric geometry cannot meet the approved behavior, record a further automatic amendment and repeat affected assurance before acceptance; do not silently weaken hold-open or choose a different operating range.
